- **redteam-mindset**: Red-team operator discipline — the mindset corrections that separate offensive testing from defensive WAPT. Built from authorized red-team work where conservative defaults caused multiple findings to be missed and one to be incorrectly retracted. Use at the START of any red-team engagement and again whenever feeling stuck or considering 'stopping' on a defended target. The single most important skill to load when scope is 'external red team' not 'bug bounty / WAPT'. ([ajtazer/heckit](https://github.com/ajtazer/heckit/tree/HEAD/skills/methodology/redteam-mindset) · ★2)
